#### [1. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Agent Orchestration & CLI Tools

* [AgentVera](https://agentvera.dev/en/?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Desktop app for macOS, Windows and Linux that runs Gemini CLI side by side with Claude Code, Codex, Copilot, Cursor and 13 more coding agents per project, each in a real terminal using your own Gemini CLI install and login. A team kanban board hands cards to agents, and an MCP manager is built in. Closed source; free plan, Pro $9.99/month.

##### Development Tools & Utilities

* [Skim (⭐16)](https://github.com/skim-md/skim) ⭐ 16 | 🐛 8 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-03 - Browser extension that renders the Markdown plans and docs Gemini CLI writes, re-rendering live as the file changes. KaTeX, Mermaid, folder view.

##### MCP Servers

* [repofit (⭐4)](https://github.com/pth2002/repofit) ⭐ 4 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-11-03 - MCP server and CLI that reads what local coding agents already keep (Gemini CLI chats and `GEMINI.md`, plus Codex, Claude Code, Cursor and others) and recommends GitHub projects for the tasks you repeat, each pick citing the session it came from. Also lists installed MCP servers and skills that no session ever called. Runs locally and uses the agent's own model, so no API key. MIT. Add with `gemini mcp add repofit uvx repofit mcp`.
* [Keenable (⭐2)](https://github.com/keenableai/keenable-antigravity) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Shell | 📅 2026-10-29 - Web search and page fetch as clean Markdown from Keenable, a search engine with its own crawler and index built for AI agents. Gemini CLI extension that adds Keenable's hosted MCP server (`search_web_pages`, `fetch_page_content`) and a web-lookup skill; keyless, no signup or API key (rate limited per IP). MIT. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/keenableai/keenable-antigravity`.
* [AlphaOSINT LinkedIn Company](https://alphaosint.com/sources/linkedin-company-api/) - Remote MCP server for Gemini CLI that returns a company's LinkedIn page details (size, industry, locations, specialties) through `https://mcp.apify.com/?tools=johnvc/linkedin-company-api`.

#### [11. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### APIs, Data, and ML

* [TimeLogic API](https://timelogicapi.com) - World time and timezone API with cryptographically signed JSON responses, so you can verify the time wasn't tampered with. Get the current time by timezone, IP address or coordinates, convert between zones, check DST changes, and do business-day date math. WorldTimeAPI-compatible endpoints for apps that used worldtimeapi.org. Free plan includes 10,000 requests/month on all endpoints.

##### Security and PKI

* [CyberShield Studio](https://cybershieldstudio.com/) - Security monitoring for e-commerce store operators and developers. Weekly scans for JavaScript changes or injection, tracking before consent, security header settings, and vulnerable client-side libraries and WordPress plugins. Free plan monitors 1 store (home page + checkout path) with 30 days of history. No credit card required.

##### Monitoring

* [Gairė](https://gaire.lt/en) - Web Change monitoring for research, price changes, out-of/in stock monitoring. Free plan up to 10 pages checked every hour, alerts by sms, email, Webhook, Slack/Discord/Telegram integrations. No card needed.

##### Forms

* [whatayarn](https://whatayarn.com/) - Online form builder. Unlimited forms and responses on Free, subject to fair use.

##### PaaS

* [EthioDeploy](https://ethiodeploy.com/) - Hosting and agentic coding platform containing Built-in Analytics, Database, GitHub CI/CD, free sub-domain, CLI, AI coding agent. Free tier allows one project 256 MB RAM / 0.5 CPU, sleeps after 30 min idle, 512 MB database and 100,000 tokens on the default model one time.

##### Issue Tracking and Project Management

* [Archboard](https://getarchboard.app/) - Architecture diagrams your team and AI agents edit live. Free plan: 1 seat, 3 diagrams, all built-in node packs and live editing with your AI agents (MCP).
* [UserJot](https://userjot.com) - Live chat, feedback boards, a roadmap, and a changelog, so everything your customers say reaches the people and agents building your product. Free plan: Unlimited Posts, Unlimited Users, 3 Admin Roles, 2 Feedback Boards, Roadmap, Changelog.

##### Analytics, Events and Statistics

* [Pug](https://pug.sh) - Open-source product analytics with autocapture, trends, funnels, retention, user flows and unified user profiles. Free plan includes 100,000 events per month and 1 year of data retention.

##### Payment and Billing Integration

* [UsageRail](https://usagerail.com/) - API keys, rate limits, quotas and usage-based billing for paid APIs, checked inside your own server with no gateway. Free up to $1,000/month billed and 1M requests/month.

#### [6. Awesome Web Security](/content/qazbnm456/awesome-web-security/README.md)

##### Fingerprint

* [Passive OS Fingerprinting: TCP Fingerprint, p0f and JA4T](https://packet.guru/blog/passive-os-fingerprinting) - How the first TCP SYN gives away the operating system family, why a proxy shows up as a mismatch with the OS the browser reports, and how p0f and JA4T record the same packet. Written by [packet.guru](https://packet.guru/).

##### JWT

* [How to revoke a JWT, from a system that does it](https://easinvy.com/notes/revoke-a-jwt) - A per-user revocation cut-off from a production multi-tenant app: why it compares against `auth_time` rather than `iat`, the whole-seconds rounding bug it shipped with, and the routes that trusted the token. Written by [Ridam Agrawal](https://ridamagrawal.com).

##### SSRF

* [Instance Metadata Abuse](https://ransomleak.com/exercises/instance-metadata-abuse/) - Shows how a server-side request forgery bug becomes cloud credential theft through the instance metadata service, and why requiring IMDSv2 session tokens with a response hop limit in the instance configuration closes the whole class of forging bugs rather than one code path, by [RansomLeak](https://ransomleak.com/).

##### Scanning / Sub Domain Enumeration

* [Crossfyre Toolchain (⭐256)](https://github.com/clickswave/crossfyre-toolchain) ⭐ 256 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-11-02 - Recon and vulnerability scanning engines in Rust for subdomains, ports, content discovery, fingerprinting, injection testing and nuclei-style templates, with one CLI and newline-delimited JSON output by [@clickswave](https://github.com/clickswave).

##### Agent Orchestration & CLI Tools

* [EndoStack (⭐3)](https://github.com/requie/endostack) ⭐ 4 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-29 - Security-first workflows for coding agents: 62 skills that threat-model changes, security-review diffs and verify results by running them. Works in Gemini CLI, Claude Code, Codex, Cursor and 11 other agents. MIT.
* [Joxo](https://joxo.ai?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Shared project for a team's coding agents. Start Gemini CLI with `joxo gemini` and it works from the same task board, decisions and handoffs as teammates' Claude Code, Codex or Cursor agents, and can ask a teammate's agent for help. Desktop app, iPhone app and a remote MCP server.

##### Commands & Extensions

* [Ponder (⭐3)](https://github.com/Quinnod345/ponder) ⭐ 4 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-03 - Gemini CLI extension and Agent Skill for multi-pass deliberation and code review: grades every file or document across 6 dimensions, runs four adversarial perspectives (skeptic, builder, dreamer, empath), and blocks early stopping until coverage reaches 1.00 and churn converges below 0.02 across two consecutive passes. Zero runtime dependencies, MIT. Install with `gemini extensions install https://github.com/Quinnod345/ponder` or `npx skills add Quinnod345/ponder -y -a gemini-cli`.

##### Education & Study Tools

* [mock-interview (⭐2)](https://github.com/georgesmomo/mock-interview) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-11-01 - Agent Skill that runs in Gemini CLI and other agents (`npx skills add georgesmomo/mock-interview`). Runs a realistic mock job interview for any role, with research on the job and real follow-up questions, then gives an honest score out of 10 and a written report.

##### MCP Servers

* [mach-gtm-mcp (⭐1)](https://github.com/verma6uc/mach-gtm-mcp) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-01 - Open-source B2B outbound sales MCP server: build ICPs, research prospects with verified buying signals, score leads (0-100), draft cold emails and LinkedIn messages (1-3-1 framework), and generate multi-touch sequences. Human approval required before any message is sent. Works with Gemini CLI and any MCP client. Install: `npx mach-gtm-mcp`.

#### [13. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### Monitoring

* [PingWise](https://pingwise.com/) - Quorum-based uptime monitoring, using 15 probes in 6 different continents. Pricing is credit based, offering 650,000 free checks per month (translates to 75 free monitors at 5-minute checks). No credit card, unlimited users.
* [RealUptime](https://realuptime.io) - Uptime checks from 4 regions every 3 minutes, a public status page with 100 subscribers, and API, CLI, Terraform and MCP access. Free for 10 monitors, commercial use allowed, no card required.

##### Crash and Exception Handling

* [RealUptime Errors](https://realuptime.io/errors) - Error tracking that works with Sentry SDKs. Free for 10,000 events a month with 14-day retention; past the cap, events are refused and counted, never billed.

##### Analytics, Events and Statistics

* [Datasense](https://usedatasense.com/) - Web analytics with JavaScript error tracking, goals, and revenue attribution. Free plan: 1 site, 5,000 events per month.

##### Screenshot APIs

* [Shotlane](https://shotlane.dev) - Screenshots, clean Markdown and PDFs of any web page, rendered in current Chrome. Hide cookie banners, ads and other popups and many more options. A free account gives you 100 credits per month, no card required.
